i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// --- Blocked, packed GEMM (BLIS-style) parallelized over 2D tiles of C ---------\n//\n// Register block of the micro-kernel: MR rows x NR columns of C are accumulated\n// in registers (NR is a multiple of the 256-bit SIMD width of 4 doubles).\nconstexpr size_t MR = 4;\nconstexpr size_t NR = 12;\n// Depth of one packed panel (K-blocking); Apack/Bpack are sized to fit in L2.\nconstexpr size_t KC = 128;\n\n// Computes acc[MR][NR] = sum_k Ap[k][:] * Bp[k][:] and stores it into the\n// mr_eff x nr_eff sub-block of C (overwrite for the first K-block, accumulate\n// afterwards).\nstatic inline void microKernel(const size_t kc, const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp,\n                               double* __restrict Cp, const size_t ldc, const bool first,\n                               const size_t mr_eff, const size_t nr_eff) {\n    double acc[MR][NR];\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < NR; ++c) {\n            acc[r][c] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        const double* __restrict b = Bp + k * NR;\n        const double* __restrict a = Ap + k * MR;\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            const double av = a[r];\n            for (size_t c = 0; c < NR; ++c) {\n                acc[r][c] += av * b[c];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (first) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < mr_eff; ++r) {\n            for (size_t c = 0; c < nr_eff; ++c) {\n                Cp[r * ldc + c] = acc[r][c];\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < mr_eff; ++r) {\n            for (size_t c = 0; c < nr_eff; ++c) {\n                Cp[r * ldc + c] += acc[r][c];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Packs A[i0:i1, pc:pc+kc] into MR-row panels: Ap[(panel * kc + k) * MR + r].\nstatic void packA(const double* __restrict A, const size_t N, const size_t i0, const size_t i1,\n                  const size_t pc, const size_t kc, double* __restrict Ap) {\n    const size_t m = i1 - i0;\n    size_t panel = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; L2.\nconstexpr size_t KC = 128;\n\n// Computes acc[MR][NR] = sum_k Ap[k][:] * Bp[k][:] and stores it into the\n// mr_eff x nr_eff sub-block of C (overwrite for the first K-block, accumulate\n//        Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\nvoid initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    \n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n vec.reserve(n);\n    double* const data = vec.data();\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        data[i] = 0.0;\n    }\n    vec.resize(n);\n}\n\nvoid the first\n// K-block, accumulate afterwards).\nstatic inline void microKernel(const size_t kc, const double* __restrict Ap, const double* __restrict Bp,\n                               double* __restrict Cp, const size_t ldc, const bool first,\n                               const size_t mr_eff, const size_t nr_eff) {\n#if defined(__AVX2__) && defined(__FMA__)\n    __m256d acc[MR][NV];\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < NV; ++c) {\n            acc[r][c] = _mm256_setzero_pd();\n        }\n    }\n\n// Unrolling this loop would spill the accumulators out       Cp[r * ldc + c] += acc[r][c];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\n// Packs A[i0:i1, pc:pc+kc] into MR-row panels: Ap[(panel * kc + k) * MR + r].\nstatic void packA(const double* {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cenre7Mo3M2mdoAQmSkdL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` are the only files changed; executable name and CLI are unchanged.\n\n**What the parallel version does**\n\n- **Cache-blocked, packed GEMM (BLIS-style)** replaces the naive `i,j,k` loops in `matrixMultiply`: A and B blocks are packed into per-thread contiguous panels, then fed to a register-blocked micro-kernel.\n- **Micro-kernel**: 6×8 block of C held in 12 AVX2 accumulators (`_mm256_fmadd_pd`), with a plain-C fallback under `#else` if the target lacks AVX2/FMA. Register blocking 6×8 measured 2.3× faster than a 4×12 variant that spilled.\n- **OpenMP decomposition**: `#pragma omp parallel` + `omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic)` over the 2-D grid of C tiles — no reductions, no shared writes, each tile is written by exactly one thread.\n- **Adaptive tile sizing**: `MC`/`NC` are picked at runtime from `omp_get_max_threads()` and `N` by scoring load balance (tiles per thread) against per-tile efficiency, subject to the packed panels fitting in L2.\n- **NUMA**: `initMatrix` is parallel, and matrices are allocated via `allocateDistributed`, which first-touches the reserved storage in parallel before `resize()` so pages spread across both sockets instead of all landing on node 0 (~+25% at 128 threads).\n- **CMakeLists**: `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)`, link `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`, added `-funroll-loops`.\n\n**Correctness**: `-v` passes for N = 1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 8, 13, 31, 64, 100, 255, 256, 511, 512, 513, 1000, 1024, 2000 (edge tiles are handled by zero-padded panels + masked stores). `-r` output was diffed against a reference run of the original serial algorithm: Sum/Min/Max/Samples agree to ~1e-15 relative — the only differences come from FP re-association, so the bitwise `Hash` line differs, as it must for any reordered summation.\n\n**Performance** (this shared 2×EPYC 7763 host, best of 3; noise ±10–30%):\n\n| N | serial original | 1 thread | 64 | 128 | 256 (default) |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 2048 | 0.17 GF | 22.6 | 592 | 464 | ~400–480 |\n| 4096 | — | 22.4 | 922 | 1227 | ~1000–1140 |\n| 6144 | — | 22.8 | 1006 | 1526 | 1410 |\n\nTwo things worth flagging: the single-thread kernel alone is ~130× the original loop nest (22 vs 0.17 GFLOPS), so the \"parallel speedup\" and the total speedup differ a lot; and at 256 threads results are ~20% below 128 threads because SMT siblings contend for L1/L2 — I did not force the thread count down to physical cores, since that would corrupt any scaling study that varies `OMP_NUM_THREADS`."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":336,"cache_read_input_tokens":117989,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":336},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"dc2586db-8689-4f6c-8d50-bec752af3434","uuid":"05d8e7b8-7c3d-48ad-9e73-1f58e103d637","timestamp":"2026-09-06T20:58:55.286Z","request_id":"req_011Cenre6QkM9kTUWhgA2yeN"}
